# naming.numeric.original-capi-scalar-publication-width

Kind: `native-observation`

## Problem statement

A primitive numeric getter must preserve the original constructor/cache purpose, actual ABI width, raw publication and independent diagnostic channel. Collapsing Boolean output to truth, treating Long as Wide, or assuming error formatting has no cache effect loses observed distinctions. This fixed original experiment measures those public primitive outputs and bounded cache/residency windows; it does not validate expression truth or application admission.

## Question

For original live objects, what are the public Int/Long/Wide/Double/Boolean results, sentinel preservation, primary cache and string-residency changes, primitive result and observable error-code changes under an actual live interpreter or the C null-interpreter route, at the measured native C ABI widths?

## Conclusion

The five original C releases each complete separate live- and null-interpreter processes with 145 raw primitive rows per route; current Jim completes 108 live primitive rows and 37 explicit API-unavailable rows in its successfully linked image. Every primitive error leaves all four output sentinels unchanged in these exact rows. All measured native ABIs report 4-byte int, 8-byte long/wide/double/pointer and CHAR_BIT=8. Tcl_UniChar is 2 bytes for C8 and 4 for C9; probe translation-unit width macros and retained library configuration remain separate. C Int converts String 2147483648 to raw -2147483648 but rejects 4294967296. LP64 Long accepts 9223372036854775808 as -9223372036854775808 and 18446744073709551615 as -1 on all five C releases. C8 Wide accepts those same positive values; C9 Wide rejects both with unchanged sentinel. C8 Long accepts the measured negative beyond-signed-width inputs with wrapped results, while C9 rejects them. String NaN Double succeeds on C8.4/current Jim but fails on C8.5/C8.6/C9, with release-specific cache/result/error metadata. A cached Jim integer 17 produces raw Boolean 17 with no string materialisation; cached 4294967296 produces raw Boolean 0. Fresh Jim String 17 instead fails Boolean parsing. C cached integer Boolean outputs are 1 in those controls, with C8.4 boolean/string conversion distinguished from later cached integer routes. Every C null-interpreter row preserves the separately seeded result and observable errorCode, although selected live error formatting can change string residency. Jim primitive errors preserve seeded public errorCode while publishing the exact diagnostic result. Complete raw rows retain every constructor/getter/code/errno/primary/string/output/result/error observation independently; they are not normalized into expression truth.

Current marked source definitions compare selected raw primitive fields and independently exercise target availability, actual host stages, original-cache transport and failure-rendering obligations. Jim cached Boolean returns its raw C integer; expression/command truth remains a separate purpose. Supported descriptive target fields are exactly CHAR_BIT=8, int=4 and long=8. C84 fresh numeric Boolean narrows reached signed long to C int before truth, while cachedWide truth stays independent. Retained original library TCL_DEFS includes TCL_WIDE_INT_IS_LONG=1 independently of the probe translation unit BUILD_MACRO0.

The public software scalar facade requires an independently issued per-object engine/Host/ABI/runtime/epoch context. Null access has no fallback issuer; weak receipts neither retain a retired object nor keep its interpreter alive. Change-and-restore cannot revive a stale issuer. Native public rows describe primitive observations only and do not grant this application ownership.

Direct C84 invalid-input primitive errors retain the existing errorCode for all five public getter kinds. An additional propagation receipt describes the later NONE update and is consumed only by the actual Runtime log or VM evaluated completion boundary. Explicit Long overflow remains independently scoped.

The current Long-octal control compares nine bounded original live08 windows without borrowing the Double/Boolean invalid-octal purpose for a modern integer-only Long failure. Two separately selected C9 Long success value/cache windows remain distinct.

Boxed primitive reads retain their actually queried numeric environment and original engine issuer; raw Boolean getter integers remain separate from semantic truth. Missing or changed-and-restored issuer/ABI refuses before output and later cache effects.

## Scope

Exactly 29 fixed constructor/input cases by five selected primitive APIs, with a fresh full-init original interpreter and held original constructor object for each supported row. C uses Tcl_GetIntFromObj/LongFromObj/WideIntFromObj/DoubleFromObj/BooleanFromObj in live and NULL routes. Jim uses public Long/Wide/Double/Boolean only with a live interpreter; all 29 Int slots and all getter slots for the unclaimed Boolean and ByteArray constructor cases are explicit unavailable records, not failures or surrogate APIs. Original image 456 Jim compilation exits 1 with missing standard link dependencies and launches no primitive process; unchanged probe/request linked image 458 records its own successful compile/version/live process. All 25 provider compile/version/execute operations and the two compiler-version records remain distinct, without an all-operation PASS. Every listed 355 original input association, prior CLI receipt, exact generated executable, source/header/config/archive/compiler/dependency and output hash is verified. Runtime VERSION and independent CLI version are separately measured; original library-build compiler/configure history and unlisted inherited environment are not invented. Primary type and string-residency/pointer-stability flags are printed immediately after the primitive call, before later public result/errorCode/input rendering observers. C8.5+ errorCode observes Tcl_GetReturnOptions then DictObjGet; C8.4/Jim observe the public global errorCode variable. Those error channels do not establish private error-code identity. input_after_observers is a later rendering and cannot describe initial raw source or pre-getter cache. Raw primitive Boolean output, expression truth and branch semantics are separate questions. Native LP64 only: wasm32, LLP64, other ABI widths and BIG-IP are not tested. No cross-object/header/frame/token identity, application Native/CABI issuer, entered worker, SSA constant, source-name admission, null-Jim route or executed Rust consumer assertion follows.

These are source/API bindings with no attached assertion outcome or inferred executable availability. Pure comparisons cover two Jim cached Boolean rows, 140 selected CLong rows, selected C90Long/Wide edges,58 C84Boolean value/cache rows and four C85+BooleanNaN live diagnostic/String windows. Actual host controls separately compare six freshJimLong errno rows and 44 selected freshC84Boolean errno rows; explicit harness resets reproduce the original request and are not getter effects. Synthetic unsupported layouts, deliberate prior ERANGE, negative 17 transport, custom NaN payloads and counted 17NULX geometry are software controls, not additional original captures. int/wideInt cache names normalize to the software integer class in the pure Long comparison; no physical native primary/header identity follows. Targetless numeric refusal does not imply source evaluation failure. Existing 1558 raw rows, 37 Jim API-unavailable rows and original failedJim compile/successful unchanged linked458 retry remain immutable. No generic counted-NUL numeric acceptance, ExprBool/NOT/conditional/logical-site equivalence, wasm32/LLP64/BIGIP/application ABI issuer, native handler/lifetime/frame or cache/result authority is inferred.

These thirteen current source controls attach no assertion outcome or executable admission. The bounded comparison covers 300 C rows and two cached Jim Boolean rows; it compares selected public outputs/cache class/String correspondence/result/errorCode, not original private header or app-issued receipt identity. Constructed null sentinel, weak lifetime, duplicate/replacement, epoch, foreign Host, no-numeric-ABI and first-cause controls are software invariants. Native456 initial failed Jim linkage and successful unchanged458 capture remain independent and immutable. No expression/NOT/condition/logical-site equivalence, external callback chronology, source entry/frame, output ABI default, current store or arbitrary setter authority is inferred.

The three current controls are unexecuted. Native comparisons are bounded to five direct original456 code/result/errorCode fields and three older independently captured Wide/Double/Boolean callback-Eval message/errorCode fields, plus the selected Long overflow code. Int/Long propagation is a software/source recipe obligation rather than an additional original process observation. Default Runtime and actual selected VM fixture publication controls grant no Native error flags, external header/result identity, scalar issuer, application admission, every error origin or expression truth parity. Original provider answers/evidence/replay and the failed independent Syntax301/302 Long comparisons remain unchanged.

This added source definition is unexecuted. Its Native field comparisons are limited to seven selected live failure code/message/errorCode/no-cache observations and two C9 Long live success value/cache-kind observations from the unchanged original456 capture. Original140 live/null comparisons, earlier1113/390 comparisons and failed Syntax301/302 assertions retain their own scope; no completed whole comparison, private object/header identity, application issuer, new ABI width, other counted/NUL bytes or expression-truth permission is inferred. Source303 predates this control.

The added scalar ABI source controls issue no new external getter observation, C ABI width certificate, original header or expression-truth result. Their actual Runtime target requires its own successful producer, inventory and assertion receipt; the separate failed Runtime311 build supplies none.

Public global errorCode is the recorded C8.4 channel; null rows retain seeded result/code.

tcl8.4 8.4.20, revision `Exact pinned original release/current distribution bytes; unrecorded source-control association is not invented.`, `/workspace/tcl-lsp/tmp/tcl8.4.20/generic/tclObj.c`, function `Tcl_GetIntFromObj`, lines 2111–2155. Full-source SHA-256 `6e20f047f13e19527f746934b7a9472405d1871e0936687004506f966bb8bd9c`; snippet SHA-256 `c26ed8667faaafbb40390cc4bf22e05e55c4f0085732a652058a8f833fed928a`; retained evidence `native_ensemble_map_prefix312-map-source-8.4.20-tclObj.c`.

```text
Tcl_GetIntFromObj(interp, objPtr, intPtr)
    Tcl_Interp *interp; 	/* Used for error reporting if not NULL. */
    register Tcl_Obj *objPtr;	/* The object from which to get a int. */
    register int *intPtr;	/* Place to store resulting int. */
{
    int result;
    Tcl_WideInt w = 0;

    /*
     * If the object isn't already an integer of any width, try to
     * convert it to one.
     */

    if (objPtr->typePtr != &tclIntType && objPtr->typePtr != &tclWideIntType) {
	result = SetIntOrWideFromAny(interp, objPtr);
	if (result != TCL_OK) {
	    return result;
	}
    }

    /*
     * Object should now be either int or wide. Get its value.
     */

#ifndef TCL_WIDE_INT_IS_LONG
    if (objPtr->typePtr == &tclWideIntType) {
	w = objPtr->internalRep.wideValue;
    } else
#endif
    {
	w = Tcl_LongAsWide(objPtr->internalRep.longValue);
    }

    if ((LLONG_MAX > UINT_MAX)
	    && ((w > UINT_MAX) || (w < -(Tcl_WideInt)UINT_MAX))) {
	if (interp != NULL) {
	    Tcl_SetObjResult(interp, Tcl_NewStringObj(
		"integer value too large to represent as non-long integer",
		-1));
	}
	return TCL_ERROR;
    }
    *intPtr = (int)w;
    return TCL_OK;
}

```

tcl8.4 8.4.20, revision `Exact pinned original release/current distribution bytes; unrecorded source-control association is not invented.`, `/workspace/tcl-lsp/tmp/tcl8.4.20/generic/tclObj.c`, function `Tcl_GetLongFromObj`, lines 2553–2595. Full-source SHA-256 `6e20f047f13e19527f746934b7a9472405d1871e0936687004506f966bb8bd9c`; snippet SHA-256 `0b5fa8c8917df7c5bec686ba41a341c368d5cb8d108da95678a988c9293bdb9a`; retained evidence `native_ensemble_map_prefix312-map-source-8.4.20-tclObj.c`.

```text
Tcl_GetLongFromObj(interp, objPtr, longPtr)
    Tcl_Interp *interp; 	/* Used for error reporting if not NULL. */
    register Tcl_Obj *objPtr;	/* The object from which to get a long. */
    register long *longPtr;	/* Place to store resulting long. */
{
    register int result;
    
    if (objPtr->typePtr != &tclIntType && objPtr->typePtr != &tclWideIntType) {
	result = SetIntOrWideFromAny(interp, objPtr);
	if (result != TCL_OK) {
	    return result;
	}
    }

#ifndef TCL_WIDE_INT_IS_LONG
    if (objPtr->typePtr == &tclWideIntType) {
	/*
	 * If the object is already a wide integer, don't convert it.
	 * This code allows for any integer in the range -ULONG_MAX to
	 * ULONG_MAX to be converted to a long, ignoring overflow.
	 * The rule preserves existing semantics for conversion of
	 * integers on input, but avoids inadvertent demotion of
	 * wide integers to 32-bit ones in the internal rep.
	 */

	Tcl_WideInt w = objPtr->internalRep.wideValue;
	if (w >= -(Tcl_WideInt)(ULONG_MAX) && w <= (Tcl_WideInt)(ULONG_MAX)) {
	    *longPtr = Tcl_WideAsLong(w);
	    return TCL_OK;
	} else {
	    if (interp != NULL) {
		Tcl_ResetResult(interp);
		Tcl_AppendToObj(Tcl_GetObjResult(interp),
			"integer value too large to represent", -1);
	    }
	    return TCL_ERROR;
	}
    }
#endif

    *longPtr = objPtr->internalRep.longValue;
    return TCL_OK;
}

```

tcl8.4 8.4.20, revision `Exact pinned original release/current distribution bytes; unrecorded source-control association is not invented.`, `/workspace/tcl-lsp/tmp/tcl8.4.20/generic/tclObj.c`, function `Tcl_GetWideIntFromObj`, lines 2946–2973. Full-source SHA-256 `6e20f047f13e19527f746934b7a9472405d1871e0936687004506f966bb8bd9c`; snippet SHA-256 `afa41bcb161dc1e6fb2864097c2096bde95138f91994be4cbe6e02aa866b406e`; retained evidence `native_ensemble_map_prefix312-map-source-8.4.20-tclObj.c`.

```text
Tcl_GetWideIntFromObj(interp, objPtr, wideIntPtr)
    Tcl_Interp *interp; 	/* Used for error reporting if not NULL. */
    register Tcl_Obj *objPtr;	/* Object from which to get a wide int. */
    register Tcl_WideInt *wideIntPtr; /* Place to store resulting long. */
{
    register int result;

    if (objPtr->typePtr == &tclWideIntType) {
    gotWide:
	*wideIntPtr = objPtr->internalRep.wideValue;
	return TCL_OK;
    }
    if (objPtr->typePtr == &tclIntType) {
	/*
	 * This cast is safe; all valid ints/longs are wides.
	 */

	objPtr->internalRep.wideValue =
		Tcl_LongAsWide(objPtr->internalRep.longValue);
	objPtr->typePtr = &tclWideIntType;
	goto gotWide;
    }
    result = SetWideIntFromAny(interp, objPtr);
    if (result == TCL_OK) {
	*wideIntPtr = objPtr->internalRep.wideValue;
    }
    return result;
}

Tcl_GetDoubleFromObj(interp, objPtr, dblPtr)
    Tcl_Interp *interp; 	/* Used for error reporting if not NULL. */
    register Tcl_Obj *objPtr;	/* The object from which to get a double. */
    register double *dblPtr;	/* Place to store resulting double. */
{
    register int result;
    
    if (objPtr->typePtr == &tclDoubleType) {
	*dblPtr = objPtr->internalRep.doubleValue;
	return TCL_OK;
    }

    result = SetDoubleFromAny(interp, objPtr);
    if (result == TCL_OK) {
	*dblPtr = objPtr->internalRep.doubleValue;
    }
    return result;
}

Tcl_GetBooleanFromObj(interp, objPtr, boolPtr)
    Tcl_Interp *interp; 	/* Used for error reporting if not NULL. */
    register Tcl_Obj *objPtr;	/* The object from which to get boolean. */
    register int *boolPtr;	/* Place to store resulting boolean. */
{
    register int result;

    if (objPtr->typePtr == &tclBooleanType) {
	result = TCL_OK;
    } else {
	result = SetBooleanFromAny(interp, objPtr);
    }

    if (result == TCL_OK) {
	*boolPtr = (int) objPtr->internalRep.longValue;
    }
    return result;
}

tcl8.5 8.5.19, revision `Exact pinned original release/current distribution bytes; unrecorded source-control association is not invented.`, `/workspace/tcl-lsp/tmp/tcl8.5.19/generic/tclObj.c`, function `Tcl_GetIntFromObj`, lines 2469–2494. Full-source SHA-256 `25136b8ad5a833dfecd5e45a2f8f54e52d78e10ff516a5abc61fbdfb7e6ee014`; snippet SHA-256 `6ef0a6f6f32d8ee82250dd59a804a3e4463ef08259bf1a3f11be3cbe9c210df7`; retained evidence `native_dict_write_error_lifetime226-failed-request225-original-source-tclObj.c`.

```text
Tcl_GetIntFromObj(
    Tcl_Interp *interp, 	/* Used for error reporting if not NULL. */
    register Tcl_Obj *objPtr,	/* The object from which to get a int. */
    register int *intPtr)	/* Place to store resulting int. */
{
#if (LONG_MAX == INT_MAX)
    return TclGetLongFromObj(interp, objPtr, (long *) intPtr);
#else
    long l;

    if (TclGetLongFromObj(interp, objPtr, &l) != TCL_OK) {
	return TCL_ERROR;
    }
    if ((ULONG_MAX > UINT_MAX) && ((l > UINT_MAX) || (l < -(long)UINT_MAX))) {
	if (interp != NULL) {
	    CONST char *s =
		    "integer value too large to represent as non-long integer";
	    Tcl_SetObjResult(interp, Tcl_NewStringObj(s, -1));
	    Tcl_SetErrorCode(interp, "ARITH", "IOVERFLOW", s, NULL);
	}
	return TCL_ERROR;
    }
    *intPtr = (int) l;
    return TCL_OK;
#endif
}

```

tcl8.5 8.5.19, revision `Exact pinned original release/current distribution bytes; unrecorded source-control association is not invented.`, `/workspace/tcl-lsp/tmp/tcl8.5.19/generic/tclObj.c`, function `Tcl_GetLongFromObj`, lines 2730–2813. Full-source SHA-256 `25136b8ad5a833dfecd5e45a2f8f54e52d78e10ff516a5abc61fbdfb7e6ee014`; snippet SHA-256 `bf087f433bc41ad74c4df2a2d232dd797728f7b6b2069ed8bb690b9d0f44771e`; retained evidence `native_dict_write_error_lifetime226-failed-request225-original-source-tclObj.c`.

```text
Tcl_GetLongFromObj(
    Tcl_Interp *interp, 	/* Used for error reporting if not NULL. */
    register Tcl_Obj *objPtr,	/* The object from which to get a long. */
    register long *longPtr)	/* Place to store resulting long. */
{
    do {
	if (objPtr->typePtr == &tclIntType) {
	    *longPtr = objPtr->internalRep.longValue;
	    return TCL_OK;
	}
#ifndef NO_WIDE_TYPE
	if (objPtr->typePtr == &tclWideIntType) {
	    /*
	     * We return any integer in the range -ULONG_MAX to ULONG_MAX
	     * converted to a long, ignoring overflow. The rule preserves
	     * existing semantics for conversion of integers on input, but
	     * avoids inadvertent demotion of wide integers to 32-bit ones in
	     * the internal rep.
	     */

	    Tcl_WideInt w = objPtr->internalRep.wideValue;
	    if (w >= -(Tcl_WideInt)(ULONG_MAX)
		    && w <= (Tcl_WideInt)(ULONG_MAX)) {
		*longPtr = Tcl_WideAsLong(w);
		return TCL_OK;
	    }
	    goto tooLarge;
	}
#endif
        if (objPtr->typePtr == &tclDoubleType) {
            if (interp != NULL) {
		Tcl_Obj *msg;

		TclNewLiteralStringObj(msg, "expected integer but got \"");
		Tcl_AppendObjToObj(msg, objPtr);
		Tcl_AppendToObj(msg, "\"", -1);
		Tcl_SetObjResult(interp, msg);
	    }
	    return TCL_ERROR;
	}
        if (objPtr->typePtr == &tclBignumType) {
	    /*
	     * Must check for those bignum values that can fit in a long, even
	     * when auto-narrowing is enabled. Only those values in the signed
	     * long range get auto-narrowed to tclIntType, while all the
	     * values in the unsigned long range will fit in a long.
	     */

	    mp_int big;

	    UNPACK_BIGNUM(objPtr, big);
	    if ((size_t)(big.used) <= (CHAR_BIT * sizeof(long) + DIGIT_BIT - 1)
		    / DIGIT_BIT) {
		unsigned long value = 0, numBytes = sizeof(long);
		long scratch;
		unsigned char *bytes = (unsigned char *)&scratch;
		if (mp_to_unsigned_bin_n(&big, bytes, &numBytes) == MP_OKAY) {
		    while (numBytes-- > 0) {
			value = (value << CHAR_BIT) | *bytes++;
		    }
		    if (big.sign) {
			*longPtr = - (long) value;
		    } else {
			*longPtr = (long) value;
		    }
		    return TCL_OK;
		}
	    }
#ifndef NO_WIDE_TYPE
	tooLarge:
#endif
	    if (interp != NULL) {
		const char *s = "integer value too large to represent";
		Tcl_Obj *msg = Tcl_NewStringObj(s, -1);

		Tcl_SetObjResult(interp, msg);
		Tcl_SetErrorCode(interp, "ARITH", "IOVERFLOW", s, NULL);
	    }
	    return TCL_ERROR;
	}
    } while (TclParseNumber(interp, objPtr, "integer", NULL, -1, NULL,
	    TCL_PARSE_INTEGER_ONLY)==TCL_OK);
    return TCL_ERROR;
}

```

Tcl_GetWideIntFromObj(
    Tcl_Interp *interp, 	/* Used for error reporting if not NULL. */
    register Tcl_Obj *objPtr,	/* Object from which to get a wide int. */
    register Tcl_WideInt *wideIntPtr)
				/* Place to store resulting long. */
{
    do {
#ifndef NO_WIDE_TYPE
	if (objPtr->typePtr == &tclWideIntType) {
	    *wideIntPtr = objPtr->internalRep.wideValue;
	    return TCL_OK;
	}
#endif
	if (objPtr->typePtr == &tclIntType) {
	    *wideIntPtr = (Tcl_WideInt) objPtr->internalRep.longValue;
	    return TCL_OK;
	}
        if (objPtr->typePtr == &tclDoubleType) {
            if (interp != NULL) {
		Tcl_Obj *msg;

		TclNewLiteralStringObj(msg, "expected integer but got \"");
		Tcl_AppendObjToObj(msg, objPtr);
		Tcl_AppendToObj(msg, "\"", -1);
		Tcl_SetObjResult(interp, msg);
	    }
	    return TCL_ERROR;
	}
        if (objPtr->typePtr == &tclBignumType) {
	    /*
	     * Must check for those bignum values that can fit in a
	     * Tcl_WideInt, even when auto-narrowing is enabled.
	     */

	    mp_int big;

	    UNPACK_BIGNUM(objPtr, big);
	    if ((size_t)(big.used) <= (CHAR_BIT * sizeof(Tcl_WideInt)
		     + DIGIT_BIT - 1) / DIGIT_BIT) {
		Tcl_WideUInt value = 0;
		unsigned long numBytes = sizeof(Tcl_WideInt);
		Tcl_WideInt scratch;
		unsigned char *bytes = (unsigned char *) &scratch;

		if (mp_to_unsigned_bin_n(&big, bytes, &numBytes) == MP_OKAY) {
		    while (numBytes-- > 0) {
			value = (value << CHAR_BIT) | *bytes++;
		    }
		    if (big.sign) {
			*wideIntPtr = - (Tcl_WideInt) value;
		    } else {
			*wideIntPtr = (Tcl_WideInt) value;
		    }
		    return TCL_OK;
		}
	    }
	    if (interp != NULL) {
		const char *s = "integer value too large to represent";
		Tcl_Obj* msg = Tcl_NewStringObj(s, -1);

		Tcl_SetObjResult(interp, msg);
		Tcl_SetErrorCode(interp, "ARITH", "IOVERFLOW", s, NULL);
	    }
	    return TCL_ERROR;
	}
    } while (TclParseNumber(interp, objPtr, "integer", NULL, -1, NULL,
	    TCL_PARSE_INTEGER_ONLY)==TCL_OK);
    return TCL_ERROR;
}

```text
Tcl_GetDoubleFromObj(
    Tcl_Interp *interp, 	/* Used for error reporting if not NULL. */
    register Tcl_Obj *objPtr,	/* The object from which to get a double. */
    register double *dblPtr)	/* Place to store resulting double. */
{
    do {
	if (objPtr->typePtr == &tclDoubleType) {
	    if (TclIsNaN(objPtr->internalRep.doubleValue)) {
		if (interp != NULL) {
		    Tcl_SetObjResult(interp, Tcl_NewStringObj(
			    "floating point value is Not a Number", -1));
		}
		return TCL_ERROR;
	    }
	    *dblPtr = (double) objPtr->internalRep.doubleValue;
	    return TCL_OK;
	}
	if (objPtr->typePtr == &tclIntType) {
	    *dblPtr = objPtr->internalRep.longValue;
	    return TCL_OK;
	}
	if (objPtr->typePtr == &tclBignumType) {
	    mp_int big;
	    UNPACK_BIGNUM( objPtr, big );
	    *dblPtr = TclBignumToDouble( &big );
	    return TCL_OK;
	}
#ifndef NO_WIDE_TYPE
	if (objPtr->typePtr == &tclWideIntType) {
	    *dblPtr = (double) objPtr->internalRep.wideValue;
	    return TCL_OK;
	}
#endif
    } while (SetDoubleFromAny(interp, objPtr) == TCL_OK);
    return TCL_ERROR;
}

tcl8.5 8.5.19, revision `Exact pinned original release/current distribution bytes; unrecorded source-control association is not invented.`, `/workspace/tcl-lsp/tmp/tcl8.5.19/generic/tclObj.c`, function `Tcl_GetBooleanFromObj`, lines 1871–1915. Tcl_GetBooleanFromObj(
    Tcl_Interp *interp, 	/* Used for error reporting if not NULL. */
    register Tcl_Obj *objPtr,	/* The object from which to get boolean. */
    register int *boolPtr)	/* Place to store resulting boolean. */
{
    do {
	if (objPtr->typePtr == &tclIntType) {
	    *boolPtr = (objPtr->internalRep.longValue != 0);
	    return TCL_OK;
	}
	if (objPtr->typePtr == &tclBooleanType) {
	    *boolPtr = (int) objPtr->internalRep.longValue;
	    return TCL_OK;
	}
	if (objPtr->typePtr == &tclDoubleType) {
	    /*
	     * Caution: Don't be tempted to check directly for the "double"
	     * Tcl_ObjType and then compare the intrep to 0.0. This isn't
	     * reliable because a "double" Tcl_ObjType can hold the NaN value.
	     * Use the API Tcl_GetDoubleFromObj, which does the checking and
	     * sets the proper error message for us.
	     */

            double d;

	    if (Tcl_GetDoubleFromObj(interp, objPtr, &d) != TCL_OK) {
		return TCL_ERROR;
	    }
	    *boolPtr = (d != 0.0);
	    return TCL_OK;
	}
	if (objPtr->typePtr == &tclBignumType) {
	    *boolPtr = 1;
	    return TCL_OK;
	}
#ifndef NO_WIDE_TYPE
	if (objPtr->typePtr == &tclWideIntType) {
	    *boolPtr = (objPtr->internalRep.wideValue != 0);
	    return TCL_OK;
	}
#endif
    } while ((ParseBoolean(objPtr) == TCL_OK) || (TCL_OK ==
	    TclParseNumber(interp, objPtr, "boolean value", NULL,-1,NULL,0)));
    return TCL_ERROR;
}

tcl8.6 8.6.18, revision `Exact pinned original release/current distribution bytes; unrecorded source-control association is not invented.`, `/workspace/tcl-lsp/tmp/tcl8.6.18/generic/tclObj.c`, function `Tcl_GetIntFromObj`, lines 2497–2530. Full-source SHA-256 `7a7ef8ec85c74581129e8f3bef939e3191a432dcb021e0a16f1cf9971049f90d`; snippet SHA-256 `4cfddacbc9224410149a646a6ebba1de6f30470180471648ccc37267f3bb054b`; retained evidence `native_ensemble_map_prefix312-map-source-8.6.18-tclObj.c`.

```text
Tcl_GetIntFromObj(
    Tcl_Interp *interp,         /* Used for error reporting if not NULL. */
    Tcl_Obj *objPtr,	/* The object from which to get a int. */
    int *intPtr)	/* Place to store resulting int. */
{
#if (LONG_MAX == INT_MAX)
    return TclGetLongFromObj(interp, objPtr, (long *) intPtr);
#else
    void *p;
    int type;

    if ((TclGetNumberFromObj(NULL, objPtr, &p, &type) != TCL_OK)
	    || (type == TCL_NUMBER_DOUBLE)) {
	if (interp != NULL) {
	    Tcl_SetObjResult(interp, Tcl_ObjPrintf(
		    "expected integer but got \"%s\"", Tcl_GetString(objPtr)));
	    Tcl_SetErrorCode(interp, "TCL", "VALUE", "INTEGER", (char *)NULL);
	}
	return TCL_ERROR;
    }
    if ((type != TCL_NUMBER_LONG) || ((ULONG_MAX > UINT_MAX)
	    && ((*(long *)p > UINT_MAX) || (*(long *)p < -(long)UINT_MAX)))) {
	if (interp != NULL) {
	    const char *s =
		    "integer value too large to represent";
	    Tcl_SetObjResult(interp, Tcl_NewStringObj(s, -1));
	    Tcl_SetErrorCode(interp, "ARITH", "IOVERFLOW", s, (char *)NULL);
	}
	return TCL_ERROR;
    }
    *intPtr = (int)*(long *)p;
    return TCL_OK;
#endif
}

Full-source SHA-256 `7a7ef8ec85c74581129e8f3bef939e3191a432dcb021e0a16f1cf9971049f90d`; snippet SHA-256 `0147915926efde0548c2837d586439141f56db2541a847a2d42904709429eb5f`; retained evidence `native_ensemble_map_prefix312-map-source-8.6.18-tclObj.c`.

```text
Tcl_GetLongFromObj(
    Tcl_Interp *interp,         /* Used for error reporting if not NULL. */
    Tcl_Obj *objPtr,	/* The object from which to get a long. */
    long *longPtr)	/* Place to store resulting long. */
{
    do {
	if (objPtr->typePtr == &tclIntType) {
	    *longPtr = objPtr->internalRep.longValue;
	    return TCL_OK;
	}
#ifndef TCL_WIDE_INT_IS_LONG
	if (objPtr->typePtr == &tclWideIntType) {
	    /*
	     * We return any integer in the range -ULONG_MAX to ULONG_MAX
	     * converted to a long, ignoring overflow. The rule preserves
	     * existing semantics for conversion of integers on input, but
	     * avoids inadvertent demotion of wide integers to 32-bit ones in
	     * the internal rep.
	     */

	    Tcl_WideInt w = objPtr->internalRep.wideValue;

	    if (w >= -(Tcl_WideInt)(ULONG_MAX)
		    && w <= (Tcl_WideInt)(ULONG_MAX)) {
		*longPtr = Tcl_WideAsLong(w);
		return TCL_OK;
	    }
	    goto tooLarge;
	}
#endif
	if (objPtr->typePtr == &tclDoubleType) {
	    if (interp != NULL) {
		Tcl_SetObjResult(interp, Tcl_ObjPrintf(
			"expected integer but got \"%s\"",
			TclGetString(objPtr)));
		Tcl_SetErrorCode(interp, "TCL", "VALUE", "INTEGER", (char *)NULL);
	    }
	    return TCL_ERROR;
	}
	if (objPtr->typePtr == &tclBignumType) {
	    /*
	     * Must check for those bignum values that can fit in a long, even
	     * when auto-narrowing is enabled. Only those values in the signed
	     * long range get auto-narrowed to tclIntType, while all the
	     * values in the unsigned long range will fit in a long.
	     */

	    mp_int big;

	    UNPACK_BIGNUM(objPtr, big);
	    if ((size_t) big.used <= (CHAR_BIT * sizeof(long) + MP_DIGIT_BIT - 1)
		    / MP_DIGIT_BIT) {
		unsigned long value = 0;
		size_t numBytes;
		long scratch;
		unsigned char *bytes = (unsigned char *) &scratch;

		if (mp_to_ubin(&big, bytes, sizeof(long), &numBytes) == MP_OKAY) {
		    while (numBytes-- > 0) {
			value = (value << CHAR_BIT) | *bytes++;
		    }
		    if (mp_isneg(&big)) {
			*longPtr = (long)(-value);
		    } else {
			*longPtr = (long)value;
		    }
		    return TCL_OK;
		}
	    }
#ifndef TCL_WIDE_INT_IS_LONG
	tooLarge:
#endif
	    if (interp != NULL) {
		const char *s = "integer value too large to represent";
		Tcl_Obj *msg = Tcl_NewStringObj(s, -1);

		Tcl_SetObjResult(interp, msg);
		Tcl_SetErrorCode(interp, "ARITH", "IOVERFLOW", s, (char *)NULL);
	    }
	    return TCL_ERROR;
	}
    } while (TclParseNumber(interp, objPtr, "integer", NULL, -1, NULL,
	    TCL_PARSE_INTEGER_ONLY)==TCL_OK);
    return TCL_ERROR;
}

	    UNPACK_BIGNUM(objPtr, big);
	    if ((size_t) big.used <= (CHAR_BIT * sizeof(Tcl_WideInt)

		Tcl_WideUInt value = 0;
		size_t numBytes;

		unsigned char *bytes = (unsigned char *) &scratch;

		if (mp_to_ubin(&big, bytes, sizeof(Tcl_WideInt), &numBytes) == MP_OKAY) {
		    while (numBytes-- > 0) {
			value = (value << CHAR_BIT) | *bytes++;
		    }
		    if (mp_isneg(&big)) {
			*wideIntPtr = (Tcl_WideInt)(-value);
		    } else {
			*wideIntPtr = (Tcl_WideInt)value;
		    }

	    if (interp != NULL) {
		const char *s = "integer value too large to represent";
		Tcl_Obj *msg = Tcl_NewStringObj(s, -1);

		Tcl_SetObjResult(interp, msg);

	    }
	    return TCL_ERROR;
	}
    } while (TclParseNumber(interp, objPtr, "integer", NULL, -1, NULL,
	    TCL_PARSE_INTEGER_ONLY)==TCL_OK);
    return TCL_ERROR;
}

#endif
	if (TclHasInternalRep(objPtr, &tclDoubleType)) {

		Tcl_SetErrorCode(interp, "TCL", "VALUE", "INTEGER", (char *)NULL);

	if (TclHasInternalRep(objPtr, &tclBignumType)) {
	    /*

		{

	    size_t numBytes;

	    TclUnpackBignum(objPtr, big);
	    if (mp_to_ubin(&big, bytes, sizeof(long), &numBytes) == MP_OKAY) {
		while (numBytes-- > 0) {
			value = (value << CHAR_BIT) | *bytes++;
		}
		if (big.sign) {
		    if (value <= 1 + (unsigned long)LONG_MAX) {
			*longPtr = (long)(-value);
			return TCL_OK;
		    }
		} else {
		    if (value <= (unsigned long)ULONG_MAX) {
			*longPtr = (long)value;
			return TCL_OK;
		    }
		}
	    }
	    }
#ifndef TCL_WIDE_INT_IS_LONG
	tooLarge:
#endif
	    if (interp != NULL) {
		const char *s = "integer value too large to represent";
		Tcl_Obj *msg = Tcl_NewStringObj(s, -1);

		Tcl_SetObjResult(interp, msg);
		Tcl_SetErrorCode(interp, "ARITH", "IOVERFLOW", s, (char *)NULL);
	    }
	    return TCL_ERROR;
	}
    } while (TclParseNumber(interp, objPtr, "integer", NULL, -1, NULL,
	    TCL_PARSE_INTEGER_ONLY)==TCL_OK);
    return TCL_ERROR;
}

tcl9.0 9.0.4, revision `Exact pinned original release/current distribution bytes; unrecorded source-control association is not invented.`, `/workspace/tcl-lsp/tmp/tcl9.0.4/generic/tclObj.c`, function `Tcl_GetWideIntFromObj`, lines 2996–3057. Full-source SHA-256 `91a390bd24fbe71108108eeccf9955df0389f0f81dff24f303c32344469e8d4a`; snippet SHA-256 `161fb571d133df7044066b27b12040539ee3591d8870eab7074a6d8d1d183ca6`; retained evidence `native_ensemble_map_prefix312-map-source-9.0.4-tclObj.c`.

```text
Tcl_GetWideIntFromObj(
    Tcl_Interp *interp,         /* Used for error reporting if not NULL. */
    Tcl_Obj *objPtr,	/* Object from which to get a wide int. */
    Tcl_WideInt *wideIntPtr)
				/* Place to store resulting long. */
{
    do {
	if (TclHasInternalRep(objPtr, &tclIntType)) {
	    *wideIntPtr = objPtr->internalRep.wideValue;
	    return TCL_OK;
	}
	if (TclHasInternalRep(objPtr, &tclDoubleType)) {
	    if (interp != NULL) {
		Tcl_SetObjResult(interp, Tcl_ObjPrintf(
			"expected integer but got \"%s\"",
			TclGetString(objPtr)));
		Tcl_SetErrorCode(interp, "TCL", "VALUE", "INTEGER", (char *)NULL);
	    }
	    return TCL_ERROR;
	}
	if (TclHasInternalRep(objPtr, &tclBignumType)) {
	    /*
	     * Must check for those bignum values that can fit in a
	     * Tcl_WideInt, even when auto-narrowing is enabled.
	     */

	    mp_int big;
	    Tcl_WideUInt value = 0;
	    size_t numBytes;
	    Tcl_WideInt scratch;
	    unsigned char *bytes = (unsigned char *) &scratch;

	    TclUnpackBignum(objPtr, big);
	    if (mp_to_ubin(&big, bytes, sizeof(Tcl_WideInt), &numBytes) == MP_OKAY) {
		while (numBytes-- > 0) {
		    value = (value << CHAR_BIT) | *bytes++;
		}
		if (big.sign) {
		    if (value <= 1 + ~(Tcl_WideUInt)WIDE_MIN) {
			*wideIntPtr = (Tcl_WideInt)(-value);
			return TCL_OK;
		    }
		} else {
		    if (value <= (Tcl_WideUInt)WIDE_MAX) {
			*wideIntPtr = (Tcl_WideInt)value;
			return TCL_OK;
		    }
		}
	    }
	    if (interp != NULL) {
		const char *s = "integer value too large to represent";
		Tcl_Obj *msg = Tcl_NewStringObj(s, -1);

		Tcl_SetObjResult(interp, msg);
		Tcl_SetErrorCode(interp, "ARITH", "IOVERFLOW", s, (char *)NULL);
	    }
	    return TCL_ERROR;
	}
    } while (TclParseNumber(interp, objPtr, "integer", NULL, -1, NULL,
	    TCL_PARSE_INTEGER_ONLY)==TCL_OK);
    return TCL_ERROR;
}

```

tcl9.0 9.0.4, revision `Exact pinned original release/current distribution bytes; unrecorded source-control association is not invented.`, `/workspace/tcl-lsp/tmp/tcl9.0.4/generic/tclObj.c`, function `Tcl_GetDoubleFromObj`, lines 2439–2471. Full-source SHA-256 `91a390bd24fbe71108108eeccf9955df0389f0f81dff24f303c32344469e8d4a`; snippet SHA-256 `ece1b2dd2579d77def8148de757b5a97fe8cc4043c7c10afd8492b86b3a700ef`; retained evidence `native_ensemble_map_prefix312-map-source-9.0.4-tclObj.c`.

```text
Tcl_GetDoubleFromObj(
    Tcl_Interp *interp,         /* Used for error reporting if not NULL. */
    Tcl_Obj *objPtr,	/* The object from which to get a double. */
    double *dblPtr)	/* Place to store resulting double. */
{
    do {
	if (TclHasInternalRep(objPtr, &tclDoubleType)) {
	    if (isnan(objPtr->internalRep.doubleValue)) {
		if (interp != NULL) {
		    Tcl_SetObjResult(interp, Tcl_NewStringObj(
			    "floating point value is Not a Number", -1));
		    Tcl_SetErrorCode(interp, "TCL", "VALUE", "DOUBLE", "NAN",
			    (char *)NULL);
		}
		return TCL_ERROR;
	    }
	    *dblPtr = (double) objPtr->internalRep.doubleValue;
	    return TCL_OK;
	}
	if (TclHasInternalRep(objPtr, &tclIntType)) {
	    *dblPtr = (double) objPtr->internalRep.wideValue;
	    return TCL_OK;
	}
	if (TclHasInternalRep(objPtr, &tclBignumType)) {
	    mp_int big;

	    TclUnpackBignum(objPtr, big);
	    *dblPtr = TclBignumToDouble(&big);
	    return TCL_OK;
	}
    } while (SetDoubleFromAny(interp, objPtr) == TCL_OK);
    return TCL_ERROR;
}

tcl9.1 9.1.0, revision `Exact pinned original release/current distribution bytes; unrecorded source-control association is not invented.`, `/workspace/tcl-lsp/tmp/tcl9.1.0/generic/tclObj.c`, function `Tcl_GetIntFromObj`, lines 2515–2540. Full-source SHA-256 `58c041dbf20bba3c8fef5782969c661d4d60a6ee0bc915f30e55c19cedb31976`; snippet SHA-256 `c4b8a5f4d0345578b50c3a393969a154a2550d4603ec08b9451857eb4f7f3e27`; retained evidence `native_ensemble_map_prefix312-map-source-9.1.0-tclObj.c`.

Tcl_GetIntFromObj(
    Tcl_Interp *interp,		/* Used for error reporting if not NULL. */
    Tcl_Obj *objPtr,		/* The object from which to get a int. */
    int *intPtr)		/* Place to store resulting int. */
{
#if (LONG_MAX == INT_MAX)
    return TclGetLongFromObj(interp, objPtr, (long *) intPtr);
#else
    long l;

    if (TclGetLongFromObj(interp, objPtr, &l) != TCL_OK) {
	return TCL_ERROR;
    }
    if ((ULONG_MAX > UINT_MAX) && ((l > UINT_MAX) || (l < INT_MIN))) {
	if (interp != NULL) {
	    const char *s =
		    "integer value too large to represent";
	    Tcl_SetObjResult(interp, Tcl_NewStringObj(s, -1));
	    Tcl_SetErrorCode(interp, "ARITH", "IOVERFLOW", s, (char *)NULL);
	}
	return TCL_ERROR;
    }
    *intPtr = (int) l;
    return TCL_OK;
#endif
}

tcl9.1 9.1.0, revision `Exact pinned original release/current distribution bytes; unrecorded source-control association is not invented.`, `/workspace/tcl-lsp/tmp/tcl9.1.0/generic/tclObj.c`, function `Tcl_GetLongFromObj`, lines 2619–2704. Full-source SHA-256 `58c041dbf20bba3c8fef5782969c661d4d60a6ee0bc915f30e55c19cedb31976`; snippet SHA-256 `c791cd5f0bf3884659e5bfc744f4ab6801f8eb60bb4e61393e305a5b4c1e1700`; retained evidence `native_ensemble_map_prefix312-map-source-9.1.0-tclObj.c`.

```text
Tcl_GetLongFromObj(
    Tcl_Interp *interp,		/* Used for error reporting if not NULL. */
    Tcl_Obj *objPtr,		/* The object from which to get a long. */
    long *longPtr)		/* Place to store resulting long. */
{
    do {
#ifdef TCL_WIDE_INT_IS_LONG
	if (TclHasInternalRep(objPtr, &tclIntType)) {
	    *longPtr = objPtr->internalRep.wideValue;
	    return TCL_OK;
	}
#else
	if (TclHasInternalRep(objPtr, &tclIntType)) {
	    /*
	     * We return any integer in the range LONG_MIN to ULONG_MAX
	     * converted to a long, ignoring overflow. The rule preserves
	     * existing semantics for conversion of integers on input, but
	     * avoids inadvertent demotion of wide integers to 32-bit ones in
	     * the internal rep.
	     */

	    Tcl_WideInt w = objPtr->internalRep.wideValue;

	    if (w >= (Tcl_WideInt)(LONG_MIN)
		    && w <= (Tcl_WideInt)(ULONG_MAX)) {
		*longPtr = (long)w;
		return TCL_OK;
	    }
	    goto tooLarge;
	}
#endif
	if (TclHasInternalRep(objPtr, &tclDoubleType)) {
	    if (interp != NULL) {
		Tcl_SetObjResult(interp, Tcl_ObjPrintf(
			"expected integer but got \"%s\"",
			TclGetString(objPtr)));
		Tcl_SetErrorCode(interp, "TCL", "VALUE", "INTEGER", (char *)NULL);
	    }
	    return TCL_ERROR;
	}
	if (TclHasInternalRep(objPtr, &tclBignumType)) {
	    /*
	     * Must check for those bignum values that can fit in a long, even
	     * when auto-narrowing is enabled. Only those values in the signed
	     * long range get auto-narrowed to tclIntType, while all the
	     * values in the unsigned long range will fit in a long.
	     */

	    mp_int big;
	    unsigned long scratch, value = 0;
	    unsigned char *bytes = (unsigned char *) &scratch;
	    size_t numBytes;

	    TclUnpackBignum(objPtr, big);
	    if (mp_to_ubin(&big, bytes, sizeof(long), &numBytes) == MP_OKAY) {
		while (numBytes-- > 0) {
		    value = (value << CHAR_BIT) | *bytes++;
		}
		if (big.sign) {
		    if (value <= 1 + (unsigned long)LONG_MAX) {
			*longPtr = (long)(-value);
			return TCL_OK;
		    }
		} else {
		    if (value <= (unsigned long)ULONG_MAX) {
			*longPtr = (long)value;
			return TCL_OK;
		    }
		}
	    }
	    goto tooLarge;
	}
    } while (TclParseNumber(interp, objPtr, "integer", NULL, -1, NULL,
	    TCL_PARSE_INTEGER_ONLY)==TCL_OK);
    return TCL_ERROR;

  tooLarge:
    if (interp != NULL) {
	const char *s = "integer value too large to represent";
	Tcl_Obj *msg = Tcl_NewStringObj(s, -1);

	Tcl_SetObjResult(interp, msg);
	Tcl_SetErrorCode(interp, "ARITH", "IOVERFLOW", s, (char *)NULL);
    }
    return TCL_ERROR;
}

```

int Jim_GetLong(Jim_Interp *interp, Jim_Obj *objPtr, long *longPtr)
{
    jim_wide wideValue;
    int retval;

    retval = Jim_GetWide(interp, objPtr, &wideValue);
    if (retval == JIM_OK) {
        *longPtr = (long)wideValue;
        return JIM_OK;
    }
    return JIM_ERR;
}

int Jim_GetWide(Jim_Interp *interp, Jim_Obj *objPtr, jim_wide * widePtr)
{
    if (objPtr->typePtr != &intObjType && SetIntFromAny(interp, objPtr, JIM_ERRMSG) == JIM_ERR)
        return JIM_ERR;
    *widePtr = JimWideValue(objPtr);
    return JIM_OK;
}

jim 0.84-9-g5bac7c9, revision `Exact pinned original release/current distribution bytes; unrecorded source-control association is not invented.`, `/workspace/.proofs/native-providers/jimtcl/jim.c`, function `Jim_GetDouble`, lines 6541–6557. Full-source SHA-256 `fe5e3bea157b898e91e6c8e50c8f9eb0e89a6aba8c6a26b5d4b7b2b03cba3867`; snippet SHA-256 `59c73d369f41088d20bfb662b837c6d30675187bab60697fb31f5949247784d6`; retained evidence `native_jim_alias_error_extent279-request-jim.c`.

```text
int Jim_GetDouble(Jim_Interp *interp, Jim_Obj *objPtr, double *doublePtr)
{
    if (objPtr->typePtr == &coercedDoubleObjType) {
        *doublePtr = JimWideValue(objPtr);
        return JIM_OK;
    }
    if (objPtr->typePtr != &doubleObjType && SetDoubleFromAny(interp, objPtr) == JIM_ERR)
        return JIM_ERR;

    if (objPtr->typePtr == &coercedDoubleObjType) {
        *doublePtr = JimWideValue(objPtr);
    }
    else {
        *doublePtr = objPtr->internalRep.doubleValue;
    }
    return JIM_OK;
}

```

Full-source SHA-256 `fe5e3bea157b898e91e6c8e50c8f9eb0e89a6aba8c6a26b5d4b7b2b03cba3867`; snippet SHA-256 `85d2a30848ba5ee421c898031082803ca1a2766f03af38a63854e84db26695ba`; retained evidence `native_jim_alias_error_extent279-request-jim.c`.

```text
static int SetBooleanFromAny(Jim_Interp *interp, Jim_Obj *objPtr, int flags)
{
    int index = Jim_FindByName(Jim_String(objPtr), jim_true_false_strings,
        sizeof(jim_true_false_strings) / sizeof(*jim_true_false_strings));
    if (index < 0) {
        if (flags & JIM_ERRMSG) {
            Jim_SetResultFormatted(interp, "expected boolean but got \"%#s\"", objPtr);
        }
        return JIM_ERR;
    }

    /* Free the old internal repr and set the new one. */
    Jim_FreeIntRep(interp, objPtr);
    objPtr->typePtr = &intObjType;
    /* 4 true values in jim_true_false_strings */
    objPtr->internalRep.wideValue = index < 4 ? 1 : 0;
    return JIM_OK;
}

```

Jim_Obj *Jim_NewIntObj(Jim_Interp *interp, jim_wide wideValue)
{
    Jim_Obj *objPtr;

    objPtr = Jim_NewObj(interp);
    objPtr->typePtr = &intObjType;
    objPtr->bytes = NULL;
    objPtr->taint = 0;
    objPtr->internalRep.wideValue = wideValue;
    return objPtr;
}

Jim_Obj *Jim_NewDoubleObj(Jim_Interp *interp, double doubleValue)
{

## Replay

Restore exact recorded absolute input paths from recorded-path-map.json and reproduce the independently recorded provider compile, CLI-version and live/null argv/environment for each physical image. The unchanged first Jim link failure is retained; linked-jim458 includes its own standard link dependencies and successful executable. Original request/probe, all raw streams, compiler/version records,355 input associations, prior build input receipts and generated observer executables remain complete. No original launcher source was captured and none is invented. New ABI/provider or replay execution is a new observation; no Rust assertion result or application issuer follows.
